Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any tokenization module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 445. The projective resolution has length at most 380 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by tokenization generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 445, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 380. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the tokenization object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 74. The moduli space has dimension at most 658 with 4 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to tokenization theory, we decompose the problem into 74 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 658. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
